## 51. UCP Address Line Components (`#981`), Identity-Linking Multi-AS Discovery & Selection (`#980`), Capability Event Webhooks (`#938`), Time-Bound Quote & Extensible `declared_fields` (`#930`/`#915`), and ACP Host-Side Spend Gate / `Server-Timing` / Feed Update ID Provenance (`#314`/`#273`/`#260`) (Stage 50)

### 51.1 UCP `dev.shopify.common.address_line_components` Destination Scope, Precedence & Non-Inference (`evaluate_ucp_address_line_components_guard`, UCP PR `#981`)

Enforces UCP PR `#981` (`dev.shopify.common.address_line_components`): restricts `address_line_components` (`street_name`, `street_number` -> `street_address`; `additional_information`, `district`, `subdistrict` -> `extended_address`) and `address_code` (independent delivery-point code such as Saudi Additional Number or UAE Makani) to Checkout `shipping_address` destinations, Checkout `payment.instruments[].billing_address`, and Order `fulfillment.expectations[].destination`, blocking non-shipping destinations (`business_location` pickup) and Cart (`ADDRESS_COMPONENTS_NON_SHIPPING_OR_CART_SCOPE_BLOCKED`); rejects `null` values on `address_line_components`, component fields, or `address_code` (`ADDRESS_COMPONENTS_NULL_VALUE_OR_UNKNOWN_KEY_BLOCKED`); enforces component precedence over flat lines in the same group; forbids businesses from splitting flat lines to infer structured components (`ADDRESS_FLAT_LINE_SPLITTING_INFERENCE_BLOCKED`); and blocks legacy invisible separator characters or composing `address_code` into street lines.

### 51.2 UCP Identity-Linking 3-Step Candidate Selection & Capability Event Webhooks (`evaluate_ucp_identity_selection_and_capability_webhook_guard`, UCP PRs `#980`, `#938`)

Enforces UCP PR `#980` (merged `2026-10-19`): evaluates each `authorization_servers` entry through Steps 2–3 (RFC 8414 §3.1 well-known insertion between host and path, fallback to `/.well-known/openid-configuration` ONLY on HTTP `404`, byte-for-byte `issuer` equality), discards failing or unregistered candidates without aborting discovery when valid candidates remain, surfaces/logs discarded entries, and rejects unaccepted AS entries in `authorization_servers` because array order is a preference rather than a security boundary. Also enforces UCP PR `#938` (`config.webhook`): restricts webhook subscriptions to pre-approved recognized platforms and signed requests over HTTPS without redirects or private IPs, requires thin `event.json` envelopes (`ucp`, `type`, `occurred_at`, `data` with IDs and `changed_sections`) on shared/broadcast capabilities, requires unfiltered `lookup_locations` confirmation before delisting `:removed` locations, and verifies RFC 9421 `Signature-Input` over `webhook-id`, `webhook-timestamp` (`<= 300s` delivery window), and `ucp-agent`.

### 51.3 UCP Time-Bound Quote Extension (`dev.ucp.common.quote`) & Reverse-DNS `declared_fields` Checkout Extensibility (`evaluate_ucp_quote_and_declared_fields_guard`, UCP PRs `#930`, `#915`)

Enforces UCP PR `#930`: reflects an accepted quote as exactly one `totals[]` entry of `type == "quote"` with non-empty `display_text`, blocks accepting or polling expired quotes (`now > expires_at`), and requires omitting buyer `proposal` and `accepted_id` on `complete_checkout`. Also enforces UCP PR `#915`: validates Reverse-DNS keys (`^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*(\.[a-z0-9_]+)+$`) in response-only `checkout.config` and unique `required_declared_fields`, enforces exact scope matching between `checkout.declared_fields` and `line_items[].declared_fields` without cross-scope movement, requires schema validation before `ready_for_complete`, and omits `config`, `required_declared_fields`, and `declared_fields` on `complete_checkout`.

### 51.4 ACP Optional Host-Side Spend Gate, `Server-Timing: acp;dur=<ms>` Hygiene & `feed_update_id` / `Product.last_update_id` Price Provenance (`evaluate_acp_host_gate_server_timing_and_feed_update_guard`, ACP PRs `#314`, `#273`, `#260`)

Enforces ACP PR `#314` (fail-closed host-side `mcpToolGate` allow/deny verification before sensitive ACP spend steps without replacing ACP `Authorization`/`Signature`/`Timestamp`/`Idempotency-Key` headers or exposing API keys in prompts), PR `#273` (`Server-Timing: acp;dur=<ms>` metric format without leaking internal database/shard/topology tokens or replacing independent client latency measurement), and PR `#260` (SEP `#259` `UpsertProductsResponse.feed_update_id` propagation to `Product.last_update_id` on touched items and verification against `suggested_price.source` at checkout).

---

## 52. UCP AP2 Payment Mandate Verification & Token Issuance (`#916`), RFC 9530 Raw-Byte `Content-Digest` (`#917`), Relational Catalog Search `purpose` & `reference_ids` (`#902`), ACP Feed Extensions & Razorpay UPI Circle (`#223`/`#216`), and AP2 `open_mandate_hash` / `checkout_hash` Entropy + Visa TAP RFC 9421 (`#279`/`#278`/`#21`) (Stage 52)

### 52.1 UCP AP2 Payment Mandate Verification at Token Issuance & RFC 9530 Raw-Byte `Content-Digest` (`evaluate_ucp_ap2_token_issuance_and_content_digest_guard`, UCP PRs `#916`, `#917`)

Enforces UCP PR `#916` (Step 3 `Payment Mandate Verification & Token Issuance`): requires presenting the AP2 Payment Mandate as a source credential (`credential.type == "ap2_payment_mandate"` with `payment_mandate` SD-JWT+kb and `checkout_jwt` Compact JWS plus `binding.type == "dev.ucp.shopping.checkout"`) to `/tokenize` without adding top-level `/tokenize` members; requires the Credential Provider to verify mandate signature, key binding, expiration, and `transaction_id` correlation against `checkout_jwt` before issuing an opaque, non-reversible token (`>= 128 bits` entropy, rejecting reversible "composite tokens"); and returns `payment_mandate_required` on `complete_checkout` when AP2 is negotiated without a mandate-derived token. Also enforces UCP PR `#917`: verifies `Content-Digest` (`sha-256=:<base64>:`) per RFC 9530 over the exact raw HTTP body bytes rather than RFC 8785 JCS-canonicalized JSON.

### 52.2 UCP Relational Catalog Search `purpose` & `reference_ids` Pairing and Self-Exclusion (`evaluate_ucp_catalog_search_purpose_and_reference_ids_guard`, UCP PR `#902`)

Enforces UCP PR `#902` (`dev.ucp.shopping.catalog.search`): validates `purpose` (`"search"`, `"recommendation"`, `"swap"`, rejecting unsupported values with HTTP 400 `unsupported_purpose` / JSON-RPC `-32602` instead of fallback browse), forbids `reference_ids` when `purpose == "search"`, requires `reference_ids` (`minItems: 1`, `uniqueItems: true`) when `purpose == "swap"`, excludes all referenced product/variant IDs and parent products on `recommendation`, and excludes referenced items and bare parent products on `swap` unless returning a distinct substitute variant (`is_variant == True` and `id != referenced_variant_id`).

### 52.3 ACP Feed Extensions JSONPath Whitelist & Razorpay UPI Circle Delegated Payment Handler (`evaluate_acp_feed_extensions_and_razorpay_upi_circle_guard`, ACP PRs `#223`, `#216`)

Enforces ACP PR `#223` (SEP `#222` `FeedMetadata.extensions` JSONPath `extends` whitelist with mandatory `https://` `schema` and `spec` URIs, rejecting undeclared extension fields on feed objects) and ACP PR `#216` (`com.razorpay.upi_circle` delegated payment handler for India's NPCI UPI Circle: requires opaque `delegate_id` `ucirc_...` without exposing raw PSP `mandate_id`, a fresh one-time `credential.type == "upi_circle_cryptogram"` from Razorpay TSP with `expires_at > now`, `currency == "inr"` with NPCI regulatory per-transaction ceiling `max_amount <= 1500000` paise / `₹15,000`, and `risk_signals == []`).

### 52.4 AP2 `open_mandate_hash` RFC 8785 JCS Conformance Vectors, High-Entropy `checkout_hash` Payload Claim & Visa TAP RFC 9421 `@signature-params` Verifier (`evaluate_ap2_open_mandate_hash_entropy_and_tap_rfc9421_guard`, AP2 PRs `#279`, `#278` & Visa TAP PR `#21`)

Enforces Google AP2 PR `#279` (`open_mandate_hash = SHA-256(JCS_RFC8785(unsigned_open_checkout_mandate_body))` computed over the unsigned claims object rather than the JWS compact string, rejecting float minor-unit encoding and preserving exact Unicode code points without NFC/NFD pre-normalization), AP2 PR `#278` (requiring `>= 128 bits` of payload entropy in the Checkout JWT so deterministic `Ed25519` signatures satisfy rainbow-table resistance), and Visa TAP PR `#21` (merchant backend RFC 9421 signature base ending with `@signature-params`, individually quoted covered components `("@authority" "@path")`, and `Ed25519` / `RSA-PSS` algorithm support).

---

## 53. UCP `ucp-schema 2.0.0` Bundled `$ref` Restoration (`#982`) & Variant `seller.logo` Non-Authority (`#962`), ACP JSON Schema ↔ OpenAPI Property Drift & `allOf` Sibling Starvation (`#310`/`#290`/`#284`/`#275`), AP2 `kb+sd-jwt+kb` Terminal `aud` Enforcement (`#369`) & `cnf.jwk` JSON Mode (`#385`/`#383`/`#384`) + Poppy `v0.2` (`#71`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0.0 `intake` Entity Capture (`#642`) + Visa TAP `x402` Checkout (`#11`) (Stage 54 / 中英双语)

### 53.1 UCP `ucp-schema 2.0.0` Bundled `$ref` Restoration & Variant `seller.logo` Non-Authority Rule (`verify_ucp_schema_bundle_and_seller_logo`, UCP PRs `#982`, `#962`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#982` (`fix(docs): restore bundled schema type links for ucp-schema 2.0.0 (#887)`): because `ucp-schema resolve --bundle` in `2.0.0` strips non-root `$id` and `$schema` metadata from inlined schemas, the bundler must walk the unbundled source schema alongside the bundled definition to restore cross-file `$ref`s across properties, array `items`, and `items.allOf` branches (`UNRESTORED_BUNDLED_CROSS_FILE_REF`), and normalize `_resolve_schema` cache keys via `Path(schema_path).resolve()` (`UNNORMALIZED_SCHEMA_CACHE_KEY`). Also enforces UCP PR `#962` (`feat(catalog): add optional seller logo`): `seller` and `seller.logo` (`common/types/media.json` with `https://` `url` and non-empty `alt_text`) are optional display metadata on catalog variants and **MUST NOT** be treated by platforms as establishing discovery or cryptographic authority binding to `/.well-known/ucp` (`SELLER_LOGO_AUTHORITY_CONFUSION_BLOCKED`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#982`（`ucp-schema 2.0.0` 打包 Schema `$ref` 恢复与缓存键规范化）：由于 `ucp-schema 2.0.0` 在 `--bundle` 内联时剥离非根 `$id` 与 `$schema`，必须对照未打包源 Schema 恢复跨文件属性、数组 `items` 与 `items.allOf` 的 `$ref` 引用（拦截退化为无链接 `object` 的 `UNRESTORED_BUNDLED_CROSS_FILE_REF`），并通过 `Path(schema_path).resolve()` 规范化缓存键。同时执行 UCP PR `#962`（商品变体可选 `seller.logo`）：校验 `common/types/media.json` 的 `https://` URL 与无障碍 `alt_text`，容忍缺省 `seller` 或仅含 `seller.name`，并严禁平台将展示用 `seller.logo` 误判为与 `/.well-known/ucp` 商业发现档案的密码学权威绑定（`SELLER_LOGO_AUTHORITY_CONFUSION_BLOCKED`）。

### 53.2 ACP JSON Schema ↔ OpenAPI Property Drift Detector & `allOf` + `additionalProperties: false` Sibling Starvation Guard (`verify_acp_schema_openapi_property_drift`, ACP PRs `#310`, `#290`, `#284`, `#275`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#310` (`Detect JSON Schema and OpenAPI property drift`): compares declared property sets of matching plain object schemas across JSON Schema `$defs` and OpenAPI `components.schemas`, failing on any unlisted `json-only` or `openapi-only` property (`UNLISTED_SCHEMA_OPENAPI_PROPERTY_DRIFT`) and failing when a `known-property-drift.txt` entry becomes stale after reconciliation (`STALE_KNOWN_DRIFT_ENTRY`). Also detects the Draft 2020-12 / OpenAPI 3.1 `allOf` + `additionalProperties: false` sibling starvation defect where `CheckoutSessionBase` (`#290`) or `DelegateAuthenticationSessionBase` (`#275`) rejects sibling `order` or `authentication_result` fields (`ALLOF_ADDITIONAL_PROPERTIES_SIBLING_STARVATION`), and detects `PaymentHandler.display_order` mis-indentation outside `properties:` (`PAYMENT_HANDLER_DISPLAY_ORDER_MISINDENTED`, `#284`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#310`（JSON Schema 与 OpenAPI 属性漂移检测器）：双向比对同名对象 Schema 的属性集合，拦截未登记的 `json-only` / `openapi-only` 漂移（`UNLISTED_SCHEMA_OPENAPI_PROPERTY_DRIFT`）及修复后未清理的陈旧白名单条目（`STALE_KNOWN_DRIFT_ENTRY`）；同时拦截 JSON Schema 2020-12 / OpenAPI 3.1 中基底 Schema 设置 `additionalProperties: false` 导致兄弟 `allOf` 分支字段（`CheckoutSessionWithOrder.order` `#290` 与 `DelegateAuthenticationSessionWithResult.authentication_result` `#275`）被饿死拒绝的缺陷（`ALLOF_ADDITIONAL_PROPERTIES_SIBLING_STARVATION`），以及 `PaymentHandler.display_order` 缩进脱离 `properties:` 的错误（`#284`）。

### 53.3 Google AP2 `kb+sd-jwt+kb` Terminal Audience Enforcement, `cnf.jwk` JSON-Mode Serialization, `verify_receipt` Callback Safety & Poppy `v0.2` DPoP Rules (`verify_ap2_kb_aud_cnf_and_poppy_dpop_rules`, AP2 PRs `#369`, `#385`, `#383`, `#384` & Poppy PR `#71`)

- **English**: Remediates the vulnerability demonstrated in Google AP2 PR `#369` (`poc_aud_not_enforced.py`): enforces `expected_aud` and `expected_nonce` on the terminal delegation hop even when the token header `typ` is `kb+sd-jwt+kb` or `kb-sd-jwt+kb` (`AP2_KB_SD_JWT_AUD_MISMATCH` / `AP2_KB_SD_JWT_NONCE_MISMATCH`). Enforces AP2 PRs `#385`/`#383` (`model_dump(exclude_none=True, mode="json")` on `ParsedToken.cnf_jwk` so enum fields such as `use="sig"` serialize as JSON strings rather than Python `Enum` objects: `AP2_CNF_JWK_ENUM_NOT_JSON_SERIALIZED`), AP2 PR `#384` (null-safe `has_reference_in_store_cb` check in `ReceiptClient.verify_receipt` with `AP2_RECEIPT_REFERENCE_NOT_IN_STORE` rejection when the callback returns `False`), and Poppy `v0.2` commit `39b35bd` (`#71`: `ES256` DPoP proof with $\ge 128$-bit `jti` on token endpoint and Session Token requests, exempting MCP Bearer token requests, revocation §4.9, and device authorization §4.6, plus mandatory `Idempotency-Key` on API write requests).
- **中文**: 修复 Google AP2 PR `#369`（`poc_aud_not_enforced.py`）揭示的重大验签漏洞：无论终端委托跳的 `typ` 为 `kb+sd-jwt` 还是携带 `cnf` 时的 `kb+sd-jwt+kb`，均强制校验 `expected_aud` 与 `expected_nonce`（`AP2_KB_SD_JWT_AUD_MISMATCH`）。同时强制执行 AP2 PR `#385`/`#383`（`ParsedToken.cnf_jwk` 使用 `mode="json"` 将 `use="sig"` 序列化为纯字符串而非 Python `Enum` 实例）、PR `#384`（`verify_receipt` 空回调安全检查与收据引用核验），以及 Poppy `v0.2` 提交 `39b35bd`（`#71`：除 MCP Bearer 换票、§4.9 吊销与 §4.6 设备授权外，所有令牌端点与会话令牌请求强制携带 `ES256` + $\ge 128\text{-bit}$ `jti` 的 DPoP 证明，且 API 写操作强制携带 `Idempotency-Key`）。

### 53.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0.0 Outbound `intake` (`τ-entity`) Policy & Visa TAP `x402` USDC on Solana/Base Checkout (`verify_tau2_intake_and_tap_x402_checkout`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PR `#642` & Visa TAP PR `#11`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0.0 (`989d328`, `#642`) `intake` callback agent policy (`data/tau2/domains/intake/agent_directed_policy.md` & `ENTITY_PROVENANCE.md`): requires explicit user consent before asking for missing form fields (`TAU2_INTAKE_MISSING_CONSENT_BEFORE_COLLECTION`), restricts collection strictly to `callback_order.missing_fields` (`UNSCHEDULED_FIELD_COLLECTION_BLOCKED`), blocks external/invented values (`EXTERNAL_OR_INVENTED_VALUE_BLOCKED`), requires silent `log_capture` prior to `submit_fields` (`MISSING_LOG_CAPTURE_BEFORE_SUBMIT`), validates `YYYY-MM-DD` dates, `H:MM AM/PM` times, and `mod-97` member ID check digits (`CODE_CHECK_DIGIT_INVALID`), enforces zero submission on early hangup (`EARLY_HANGUP_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED`), and forbids calling `submit_fields` and `end_call` in the same turn (`SUBMIT_AND_END_CALL_SAME_TURN_BLOCKED`). Also enforces Visa TAP PR `#11` machine-to-machine `x402` checkout (`PAYMENT-REQUIRED` header on Mode A HTTP 402 and facilitator settlement + `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` header on Mode B across Solana `solana:EtWTRABZaYq6iMfeYKouRu166VU2xqa1` and Base Sepolia `eip155:84532`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0.0（`989d328`, `#642`）外呼实体采集域（`τ-entity` `intake`）确定性策略：在询问缺失表单字段前必须先取得受访者明确同意（`TAU2_INTAKE_MISSING_CONSENT_BEFORE_COLLECTION`）、严禁采集工单列表以外字段（`UNSCHEDULED_FIELD_COLLECTION_BLOCKED`）或编造通话外数值（`EXTERNAL_OR_INVENTED_VALUE_BLOCKED`）、提交前必须逐字段调用 `log_capture`、校验 `YYYY-MM-DD` 日期/`H:MM AM/PM` 时间与会员号 `mod-97` 校验位（`CODE_CHECK_DIGIT_INVALID`）、通话提前挂断严禁提交（`EARLY_HANGUP_SUBMISSION_BLOCKED`），且 `submit_fields` 与 `end_call` 严禁同轮并发调用（`SUBMIT_AND_END_CALL_SAME_TURN_BLOCKED`）。同时强制执行 Visa TAP PR `#11` 的 `x402` 机器对机器结账护栏（Mode A 缺省签名头返回 HTTP 402 与 `PAYMENT-REQUIRED` 头，Mode B 校验 Solana/Base USDC 链上 Facilitator 清算与 `PAYMENT-RESPONSE` 回执及 `subtotal + tax + shipping == total` 守恒）。

---

## 54. Stage 55 16-Scenario Bilingual (CN/EN) 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`SD-01..SD-16`) (Stage 55 / 中英双语)

- **English**: Evaluates 16 end-to-end scenarios (`SD-01` through `SD-16`) in [`lab/ucp_schema_acp_drift_ap2_aud_tau2_intake_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_schema_acp_drift_ap2_aud_tau2_intake_bench.py) across all four Stage 54 guard domains, generating both Chinese ([`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/index.html)) and English ([`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/index.en.html)) 7-Card 4:5 visual decks backed by [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/report.json). The AI-Native 6-Plane pipeline achieves **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** perturbation stability at **0.41 ms** mean latency (**5.1x** faster than the **2.09 ms** Classic 3-Tier baseline at **1/16, 6.2%**).
- **中文**: 在 [`lab/ucp_schema_acp_drift_ap2_aud_tau2_intake_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_schema_acp_drift_ap2_aud_tau2_intake_bench.py) 中构建覆盖 Stage 54 四大协议域的 16 场景（`SD-01..SD-16`）基准评测，同步生成中文版（[`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/index.html)）与英文版（[`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/index.en.html)）7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告及密封数据 [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-schema-acp-drift-ap2-aud-tau2-intake-bench/report.json)。AI-Native 6-Plane 流水线实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 扰动稳定性，平均内联延迟 **0.41 ms**（较经典三层基线 **2.09 ms / 1/16 6.2%** 提速 **5.1x**）。

## 55. UCP RFC 9728/8414 Multi-Entry Identity Discovery & Checkout Lifecycle (`#891`/`#573`/`#672`), Mercado Pago Pix `render_artifact` (`#635`) & Razorpay Magic `hosted_checkout` (`#316`), ACP Delegate Auth `Signature` & Unwrapped `Error` Envelope (`#287`/`#289`/`#266`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `loan_servicing` (EN/FR) & Timestamp Canonicalization (`#638`/`#634`) (Stage 56 / 中英双语)

### 55.1 UCP RFC 9728/8414 Multi-Entry Identity Discovery, Fulfillment `totals` Array & Checkout Lifecycle (`verify_ucp_rfc9728_identity_linking_and_checkout_lifecycle`, UCP PRs `#891`, `#573`, `#672`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#891` (`docs(identity-linking): clarify RFC 9728 authorization_servers issuer rules and multi-entry discovery`): every entry in `authorization_servers` must be an RFC 8414 §2 issuer identifier whose discovered metadata `issuer` matches byte-for-byte with zero trailing-slash normalization (`UCP_RFC8414_ISSUER_NORMALIZATION_FORBIDDEN`); Step 2 inserts `/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server` between host and path (`UCP_RFC8414_WELL_KNOWN_APPENDED_INSTEAD_OF_INSERTED`) and allows OIDC fallback (`/.well-known/openid-configuration`) **only** on HTTP `404` (`UCP_RFC8414_NON_404_FALLBACK_FORBIDDEN`); multi-entry `Selection` discards failing/unregistered entries while logging them (`UCP_DISCARDED_AS_ENTRIES_NOT_SURFACED`) and selects the first valid candidate in array order. Also enforces UCP PR `#573` (`options[].totals` non-null array without scalar `total` or redundant title/pricing in `description`) and PR `#672` (`ready_for_complete` transitions to `requires_escalation` and `incomplete`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#891`（RFC 9728 `authorization_servers` 与 RFC 8414 多发证方发现规范）：每个候选条目必须与元数据 `issuer` 字段字节级完全一致，严禁去尾斜杠归一化（`UCP_RFC8414_ISSUER_NORMALIZATION_FORBIDDEN`）；Step 2 将 `/.well-known/oauth-authorization-server` 插入主机与路径之间，且仅在 HTTP `404` 时允许回退至 OIDC `/.well-known/openid-configuration`（非 404 错误严禁回退：`UCP_RFC8414_NON_404_FALLBACK_FORBIDDEN`）；多条目 `Selection` 按数组顺序优选首个有效且已注册的候选方，同时显式记录被丢弃条目。并强制执行 UCP PR `#573`（`options[].totals` 非空数组、禁标量 `total`、禁 `description` 重复标题）与 PR `#672`（恢复 `ready_for_complete` 至 `requires_escalation` 与 `incomplete` 的状态转换）。

### 55.2 UCP Out-of-Band Render Artifact (`com.mercadopago.payment.render_artifact` Pix) & Redirect Hosted Checkout (`com.razorpay.magic_checkout`) (`verify_ucp_render_artifact_and_hosted_checkout_guard`, UCP PRs `#635`, `#316`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#635` (`com.mercadopago.payment.render_artifact` for in-session out-of-band payment methods such as Brazil Pix QR / `copia e cola`): forbids `continue_url` redirects (`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_FORBIDS_CONTINUE_URL_REDIRECT`), forbids `required` on the action instance (`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_INSTANCE_REQUIRED_FIELD_FORBIDDEN`), requires `config.expires_at <= checkout.expires_at` (`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_EXPIRY_EXCEEDS_CHECKOUT`), restricts `image` to static `https://` or `data:image/` bytes (`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_UNSAFE_IMAGE_URI`), restricts `instructions_url` to the handler's `https://` origin allowlist without auto-submitting buyer data (`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTIONS_ORIGIN`), and blocks any PAN/OTP/CVV leakage. Also enforces UCP PR `#316` (`com.razorpay.magic_checkout` `hosted_checkout_instrument` & `hosted_checkout_credential`): forbids platform credential acquisition (`UCP_HOSTED_CHECKOUT_CREDENTIAL_ACQUISITION_FORBIDDEN`), requires `https://` `return_url` distinct from `continue_url`, forbids sensitive card/token fields, and requires authoritative checkout polling after a `cancel_url` redirect (`UCP_HOSTED_CHECKOUT_CANCEL_WITHOUT_AUTHORITATIVE_POLL`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#635`（`com.mercadopago.payment.render_artifact` 巴西 Pix 等会话内带外支付展示构件）：严禁下发 `continue_url` 重定向（`UCP_RENDER_ARTIFACT_FORBIDS_CONTINUE_URL_REDIRECT`）、严禁在动作实例上设置 `required` 字段、强制构件 `expires_at` 不晚于结账会话 `expires_at`、限制 `image` 为静态 `https://` 或 `data:image/` 图像、限制 `instructions_url` 属于处理器 `https://` 同源白名单且禁自动提交买方数据，并拦截任何 PAN/OTP/CVV 敏感凭证。同时强制执行 UCP PR `#316`（Razorpay Magic Checkout `hosted_checkout` 托管结账）：严禁平台执行令牌化/凭证获取（`UCP_HOSTED_CHECKOUT_CREDENTIAL_ACQUISITION_FORBIDDEN`）、强制 `return_url` 为 `https://` 且不等于 `continue_url`，并在买方重定向至 `cancel_url` 后强制轮询结账会话以获取权威状态。

### 55.3 ACP Delegate Authentication `Signature` Requirement, `CheckoutSessionCreateRequest` Conformance & Unwrapped `delegate_payment` Error Envelope (`verify_acp_delegate_auth_signature_and_error_envelope`, ACP PRs `#287`, `#289`, `#266`/`#243`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#287` (marking `Signature` `required: true` on `openapi.delegate_authentication.yaml` per RFC §3.1 so card/3DS endpoints never accept unsigned bearer requests: `ACP_DELEGATE_AUTH_SIGNATURE_MISSING`), PR `#289` (`CheckoutSessionCreateRequest` requires `line_items`, `currency`, and `capabilities`, rejecting legacy `items` and `product_id`: `ACP_CHECKOUT_CREATE_LEGACY_ITEMS_FIELD_REJECTED`), and PRs `#266`/`#243` (rejecting `{ "error": { ... } }` wrapped envelopes on `delegate_payment` in favor of top-level `Error` fields `type`, `code`, `message` with `additionalProperties: false`: `ACP_DELEGATE_PAYMENT_WRAPPED_ERROR_ENVELOPE_REJECTED`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#287`（按 RFC §3.1 在 `openapi.delegate_authentication.yaml` 中将分离式 `Signature` 头设为必填 `required: true`，防止接收卡号与签发 3DS 会话的端点仅凭静态 Bearer Key 放行）、PR `#289`（`CheckoutSessionCreateRequest` 必填 `line_items`/`currency`/`capabilities` 并拒绝旧版 `items`/`product_id`）及 PR `#266`/`#243`（拒绝 `delegate_payment` 错误响应中的 `{ "error": { ... } }` 外层包装，强制顶层 `type`/`code`/`message` 符合 `additionalProperties: false`）。

### 55.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `loan_servicing` (`en`/`fr` Boréal Finance) Policy & `banking_knowledge` `canonicalize_time_verified` (`verify_tau2_loan_servicing_and_time_canonicalization`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#638`, `#634`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#638` (`loan_servicing` and `loan_servicing_fr` Boréal Finance consumer-loan domain): 3-factor caller verification (`full_name`, `date_of_birth`, `postal_code`; transferring with `reason="failed_verification"` after 2 failed attempts: `TAU2_LOAN_DOUBLE_VERIFICATION_FAILURE_MUST_TRANSFER`), `authorized_third_party` read-only restriction (`TAU2_LOAN_THIRD_PARTY_WRITE_OR_DOC_FORBIDDEN`), explicit `"yes"` confirmation before every write tool (`TAU2_LOAN_WRITE_WITHOUT_CONFIRMATION`), single tool call per turn with no simultaneous borrower message (`TAU2_LOAN_TOOL_AND_MESSAGE_SAME_TURN_FORBIDDEN`), processing eligible late-fee waivers ($\le \$50.00$, no waiver in 12 months, `current`/`past_due_30`) **before** scheduling payments (`TAU2_LOAN_PAYMENT_BEFORE_WAIVER_ORDER_VIOLATION`), and forbidding any document dispatch (`statement`, `payoff_letter`, `tax_summary`) in the same conversation after an email address change (`TAU2_LOAN_DOC_AFTER_EMAIL_CHANGE_BLOCKED`). Also enforces PR `#634` (`canonicalize_tau2_banking_time_verified`): normalizes equivalent representations of `2025-11-14 03:40:00 EST` while leaving fabricated minutes or non-EST zones (`Z`, `+00:00`, `PST`) unchanged so invalid timestamps fail evaluation (`TAU2_TIME_VERIFIED_FABRICATED_OR_WRONG_TZ`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#638`（Boréal Finance 英/法双语消费信贷服务域 `loan_servicing` / `loan_servicing_fr`）：三要素核身（姓名、生日、邮编，连续两次核验失败必须以 `reason="failed_verification"` 转人工）、授权第三方仅限一般账务只读查询（严禁执行写操作或发送文档 `TAU2_LOAN_THIRD_PARTY_WRITE_OR_DOC_FORBIDDEN`）、每项写操作前必须获得明确 `"yes"` 确认且严禁与用户消息同轮调用、同一通话既申请滞纳金豁免又还款时必须**先豁免后扣款**（`TAU2_LOAN_PAYMENT_BEFORE_WAIVER_ORDER_VIOLATION`），以及当次会话修改注册邮箱后严禁在同一会话发送任何账单/结清证明/税务文档（`TAU2_LOAN_DOC_AFTER_EMAIL_CHANGE_BLOCKED`）。同时执行 PR `#634`（`canonicalize_tau2_banking_time_verified`）：将等价于固定时钟 `2025-11-14 03:40:00 EST` 的格式变体归一化，同时保留伪造分钟或非 EST 时区（`Z`/`+00:00`/`PST`）原样报错。

## 56. Stage 57 16-Scenario Bilingual (CN/EN) 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`RP-01..RP-16`) (Stage 57 / 中英双语)

- **English**: Evaluates 16 end-to-end scenarios (`RP-01` through `RP-16`) in [`lab/ucp_rfc9728_pix_razorpay_acp_envelope_tau2_loan_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_rfc9728_pix_razorpay_acp_envelope_tau2_loan_bench.py) across all four Stage 56 guard domains, generating both Chinese ([`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/index.html)) and English ([`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/index.en.html)) 7-Card 4:5 visual decks backed by [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/report.json). The AI-Native 6-Plane pipeline achieves **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** perturbation stability at **0.38 ms** mean latency (**5.4x** faster than the **2.06 ms** Classic 3-Tier baseline at **1/16, 6.2%**).
- **中文**: 在 [`lab/ucp_rfc9728_pix_razorpay_acp_envelope_tau2_loan_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_rfc9728_pix_razorpay_acp_envelope_tau2_loan_bench.py) 中构建覆盖 Stage 56 四大协议域的 16 场景（`RP-01..RP-16`）基准评测，同步生成中文版（[`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/index.html)）与英文版（[`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/index.en.html)）7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告及密封数据 [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-rfc9728-pix-razorpay-acp-envelope-tau2-loan-bench/report.json)。AI-Native 6-Plane 流水线实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 扰动稳定性，平均内联延迟 **0.38 ms**（较经典三层基线 **2.06 ms / 1/16 6.2%** 提速 **5.4x**）。

## 57. UCP Purchase Options (`#901`) & Per-Seller Catalog `offers[]` Attribution (`#811`/`#933`/`#831`), ACP Checkout `Item.url` Disambiguation & Feed Bounds (`#281`/`#256`/`#241`), Google AP2 Multi-Hop `~~` `present()` Isolation & Atomic Consumption (`#363`/`#359`/`#347`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 6-Language Voice Benchmarks & Crossed Pass³ Submission Gate (`#637`/`#639`/`#620`) (Stage 58 / 中英双语)

### 57.1 UCP Purchase Options Extension, Per-Seller Variant `offers[]` Attribution, HTTP `304` Conditional Cache & MCP `$defs/agent` Guard (`verify_ucp_purchase_options_and_seller_offers_guard`, UCP PRs `#901`, `#811`, `#933`, `#831`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#901` (`dev.ucp.shopping.purchase_options`): `purchase_options[]` is response-only (`ucp_request: "omit"`; `UCP_PURCHASE_OPTIONS_RESPONSE_ONLY_FIELD_IN_REQUEST`), `selected_purchase_option_id` and `purchase_options` are co-present on cart/checkout lines (`dependentRequired`; `UCP_LINE_ITEM_PURCHASE_OPTIONS_DEPENDENT_REQUIRED_VIOLATION`), `selected_purchase_option_id` is omitted on `complete` (`UCP_SELECTED_PURCHASE_OPTION_OMIT_ON_COMPLETE`), stale option selections fall back to `purchase_options[0]` with a mandatory `purchase_option_changed` warning (`UCP_STALE_PURCHASE_OPTION_SILENT_SUBSTITUTION_FORBIDDEN`), deferred-payment free trials must not use `price: 0` (`UCP_DEFERRED_TRIAL_MUST_NOT_USE_ZERO_OPTION_PRICE`), and Order lines freeze `{id, title}` in `purchase_option`. Enforces UCP PR `#811` (`offers[]` per-seller offer attribution): `offers[].seller.domain` must be a bare hostname identifying the Merchant of Record (`UCP_OFFER_SELLER_DOMAIN_NOT_BARE_HOSTNAME`), platforms must pass `offers[].item_id` rather than `variant.id` at checkout (`UCP_MULTI_SELLER_VARIANT_ID_USED_AT_CHECKOUT`), and `offers[].price` supersedes display-only `variant.price`. Also enforces PR `#933` (allowing `304 Not Modified` on conditional requests while blocking `301`/`302`/`303`/`307`/`308` redirects: `UCP_FORBIDDEN_HTTP_REDIRECT`) and PR `#831` (`$defs/agent` replacing reserved `ucp_`-prefixed `$defs/ucp_agent` `W007` in `mcp_tool_call.json`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#901`（`dev.ucp.shopping.purchase_options` 购买选项扩展）：`purchase_options[]` 仅限响应下发（请求体携带即拦截 `UCP_PURCHASE_OPTIONS_RESPONSE_ONLY_FIELD_IN_REQUEST`）、购物车与结账响应行必须同时携带 `purchase_options` 与 `selected_purchase_option_id`（`dependentRequired`）、`complete` 阶段严禁上送 `selected_purchase_option_id`、所选选项失效时必须回退至默认首项并显式附加 `purchase_option_changed` 警告（严禁静默替换 `UCP_STALE_PURCHASE_OPTION_SILENT_SUBSTITUTION_FORBIDDEN`）、延期付款型免费试用严禁将选项 `price` 标为 `0`（`UCP_DEFERRED_TRIAL_MUST_NOT_USE_ZERO_OPTION_PRICE`），并在订单行 `purchase_option` 中固化 `{id, title}` 快照。强制执行 UCP PR `#811`（多卖家目录变体 `offers[]` 归因）：`offers[].seller.domain` 必须为不含协议与路径的裸域名以标识记账商户（MoR）、结账必须使用所选报价的 `offers[].item_id` 而严禁传 `variant.id`（`UCP_MULTI_SELLER_VARIANT_ID_USED_AT_CHECKOUT`），且以 `offers[].price` 作为权威结算单价。同时强制执行 PR `#933`（允许条件请求返回 `304 Not Modified` 复用缓存，严禁 `301`/`302`/`303`/`307`/`308` 重定向）与 PR `#831`（将 `mcp_tool_call.json` 中的 `$defs/ucp_agent` 改为 `$defs/agent`，拦截违反 `W007` 保留前缀规则的定义名）。

### 57.2 ACP Checkout `Item.url` Storefront Disambiguation, Agent-Hosted Feed Origin, `minItems: 1` Bounds & `UpsertProductsResponse` (`verify_acp_item_url_and_feed_upsert_guard`, ACP PRs `#281`, `#256`, `#241`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#281` (SEP `#280` optional `url` on request-side `Item` in `line_items` on checkout session create/update): validates `https://` product page URIs and requires `Item.url` whenever a cross-listed SKU spans multiple brands or storefronts under a single seller account (`ACP_CROSS_LISTED_SKU_REQUIRES_ITEM_URL` / `ACP_ITEM_URL_STOREFRONT_MISMATCH`). Also enforces ACP PR `#256` (Feed API OpenAPI server URL must be agent-hosted such as `https://agent.example.com` rather than merchant-hosted `https://merchant.example.com`: `ACP_FEED_SERVER_MUST_BE_AGENT_HOSTED`; `UpsertProductsRequest.products` and `Product.variants` must both satisfy `minItems: 1`) and PR `#241` (`UpsertProductsResponse` schema `{id: string, accepted: boolean}` in `schema.feed.json`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#281`（SEP `#280` 结账创建/更新请求行 `Item.url` 规范商品页 URL）：校验 `https://` 商品链接格式，当同一卖家账户下多个品牌或店面共用同一 SKU 标识时强制通过 `Item.url` 解析目标品牌店面（缺失或店面不匹配即拦截 `ACP_CROSS_LISTED_SKU_REQUIRES_ITEM_URL` / `ACP_ITEM_URL_STOREFRONT_MISMATCH`）。同时强制执行 ACP PR `#256`（Product Feed API 服务端地址必须为代理端主机 `https://agent.example.com`，拒绝误配为商户端主机 `ACP_FEED_SERVER_MUST_BE_AGENT_HOSTED`；强制 `UpsertProductsRequest.products` 与 `Product.variants` 满足 `minItems: 1`）与 PR `#241`（校验 Feed JSON Schema 打包文件中的 `UpsertProductsResponse` `{id, accepted}` 响应结构）。

### 57.3 Google AP2 Multi-Hop `~~` `MandateClient.present()` Isolation, Mandatory `checkout_hash` Derivation & Atomic Consume-Once Reservation (`verify_ap2_multihop_present_and_atomic_consumption_guard`, AP2 PRs `#363`, `#359`, `#347`)

- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#363` (`MandateClient.present()` multi-hop `~~` delegation chains): isolates the last hop via `rpartition("~~")`, applies selective-disclosure redaction (`claims_to_disclose` while always retaining `cnf`) only to the last hop, computes `sd_hash` over the post-redaction hop (`previous_hop_to_join`), preserves all earlier hops verbatim on the wire (`f"{prior_hops}~~{previous_hop_to_join}~~{pres_jwt}"`), and rejects any tampered prior-hop disclosure (`AP2_PRIOR_HOP_DISCLOSURE_TAMPERED`). Enforces AP2 PR `#359` (`CheckoutMandateChain.verify()`): always derives `presented_checkout_hash = compute_sha256_b64url(checkout_jwt)` from the presented Checkout JWT and compares it against `closed_mandate.checkout_hash` even when `expected_checkout_hash` is omitted (`AP2_PRESENTED_CHECKOUT_HASH_MISMATCH`). Enforces AP2 PR `#347` (atomic `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` + `synchronous=FULL` consume-once reservation at the Credential Provider and Merchant Payment Processor, checking legacy token-store keys, failing closed with `replay_state_unavailable` on unhealthy reservation state, and returning `mandate_already_used` on replay).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#363`（`MandateClient.present()` 多跳 `~~` 委托链处理）：通过 `rpartition("~~")` 精确隔离末跳 `SD-JWT`，仅对末跳执行选择性披露裁剪（保留 `cnf`）并基于裁剪后令牌计算 `sd_hash`，在传输链中完整保留前置跳（`f"{prior_hops}~~{previous_hop_to_join}~~{pres_jwt}"`）且拦截任何前置跳披露篡改（`AP2_PRIOR_HOP_DISCLOSURE_TAMPERED`）。强制执行 AP2 PR `#359`（`CheckoutMandateChain.verify()` 即使调用方未传 `expected_checkout_hash` 参数，也必须从实际呈递的 `checkout_jwt` 计算 `compute_sha256_b64url` 并与闭式授权 `checkout_hash` 比对，阻断结账载荷替换 `AP2_PRESENTED_CHECKOUT_HASH_MISMATCH`）。强制执行 AP2 PR `#347`（凭证提供方与商户支付处理器通过原子单次预留锁拦截重放，兼顾旧版 token-store 记录，预留库异常时以 `replay_state_unavailable` 故障闭合，重复消费返回 `mandate_already_used`）。

### 57.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 6-Language `voice-v2-banking` / `voice-v2-entity` Benchmarks, Crossed Pass³ `voice-v2-rcs-v3` Seeds & Judged Submission Gate (`verify_tau2_voice_v2_banking_entity_and_crossed_pass3_guard`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#637`, `#639`, `#620`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#637` (`voice-v2-banking` `banking_knowledge_97` across `en/es/pt/hi/zh/ko` = 582 calls and `voice-v2-entity` `intake_200` across 6 languages = 1,200 calls with a 240s call limit and per-cell `binding_revision` SHA-256 pins: `TAU2_CELL_BINDING_REVISION_PIN_MISMATCH` / `TAU2_ENTITY_INTAKE_240S_CALL_LIMIT_EXCEEDED`), PR `#639` (`voice-v2-rcs-v3` Crossed Pass³ whole-run seeds `(42, 401, 16319)` rejecting legacy `(9401, 9402, 9403)`, read-only reuse of validated seed-42 `--regular-results`, and requiring `google-genai >= 2.26` when Gemini 3.8 extended thinking is enabled), and PR `#620` (`tau2 submit prepare/validate` refusing any `voice-v2` matrix with missing/errored/unscored rewards or unjudged applicable measures, requiring `benchmark-diagnostics.json`, and verifying recomputed verdict fingerprints).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#637`（发布跨 `en/es/pt/hi/zh/ko` 六语种的 `voice-v2-banking` `banking_knowledge_97` 共 `582` 通电话与 `voice-v2-entity` `intake_200` 共 `1,200` 通电话及 `240s` 通话上限，并校验每语种单元的 `binding_revision` 锁定哈希）、PR `#639`（Crossed Pass³ 升级至 `voice-v2-rcs-v3` 种子组合 `(42, 401, 16319)` 并拒绝旧版 `(9401, 9402, 9403)`、仅允许只读复用 seed-42 `--regular-results`、且在开启 Gemini 3.8 扩展思考时强制 `google-genai >= 2.26`）与 PR `#620`（`tau2 submit prepare/validate` 拦截任何存在缺失/报错/未评分通话或未完成适用裁判指标的 `voice-v2` 矩阵，强制生成并重算校验 `benchmark-diagnostics.json` 裁决指纹）。

## 59. UCP Root-Array Anti-Pattern (`#948`) & Order Directional Annotation Hygiene (`#903`/`#900`/`#882`/`#869`), ACP Checkout Request `LineItem` Alignment & Flat Delegate Auth Result Schema (`#270`/`#261`/`#254`/`#252`), Google AP2 Versioned `.1` VCT Constants, `PaymentInstrument` Extension Preservation & `~~` Chain Decoding (`#349`/`#333`/`#329`/`#351`/`#365`/`#364`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Design API Units (`guidance_scale=5`) & Inline Text-Matrix Trial Counting (`#636`/`#619`/`#616`/`#645`/`#632`) (Stage 60 / 中英双语)

### 59.1 UCP Root-Array Schema Prohibition, Order Directional Annotation Hygiene, Vendor Self-Hosting & Auth-Gated Stored State (`verify_ucp_root_array_order_annotations_and_auth_state`, UCP PRs `#948`, `#903`, `#900`, `#882`, `#869`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#948` (`docs: document root-array schema anti-pattern in schema authoring guide`): capability, extension, and operation payload schemas **MUST NOT** declare `"type": "array"` at the root (`UCP_ROOT_ARRAY_SCHEMA_FORBIDDEN`), because root arrays block non-breaking sibling metadata/pagination fields, prevent `allOf` property composition by extensions, and cannot host the ambient `ucp` object member. Enforces UCP PR `#903` (`fix(schema): remove stray directional annotations from order schema`): `Order` (`dev.ucp.shopping.order`) is a business-emitted snapshot with no client create/update operations; stray `"ucp_request": "omit"` on `currency`, `policies`, or `attribution` strips those fields when OpenAPI 3.1 `order_event_webhook` resolves `order.json` under `requestBody` (`UCP_ORDER_STRAY_DIRECTIONAL_ANNOTATION_FORBIDDEN` / `UCP_ORDER_WEBHOOK_REQUIRED_FIELDS_STRIPPED`). Also enforces PR `#882` (vendor/organization `com.{vendor}.*` and `org.{org}.*` schemas must be self-hosted: `UCP_VENDOR_EXTENSION_MUST_BE_SELF_HOSTED`), PR `#869` (business-populated `buyer`, `payment.instruments[]`, and `loyalty` require a user-authenticated and authorized request: `UCP_UNAUTHENTICATED_STORED_USER_STATE_LEAK_FORBIDDEN`), and PR `#900` (`--export-corpus examples.json` requires complete examples validated against composed extension schemas).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#948`（禁止根数组 Schema 反模式）：能力、扩展与操作载荷 Schema 严禁在根级使用 `"type": "array"`（`UCP_ROOT_ARRAY_SCHEMA_FORBIDDEN`），否则无法在不破坏线级结构的前提下新增分页/汇总同级字段、无法通过 `allOf` 叠加扩展属性、且无法承载保留的 `ucp` 对象成员。强制执行 UCP PR `#903`（清除 `order.json` 游离方向注解）：`Order` 为商家单向推送的状态快照，游离的 `"ucp_request": "omit"` 会导致 OpenAPI 3.1 `order_event_webhook` 在解析 `requestBody` 时剥离必填的 `currency`、`policies` 与 `attribution`（`UCP_ORDER_STRAY_DIRECTIONAL_ANNOTATION_FORBIDDEN`）。同时强制执行 PR `#882`（`com.{vendor}.*`/`org.{org}.*` 厂商扩展必须自托管于自有域名）、PR `#869`（商家回填 `buyer`/`payment.instruments[]`/`loyalty` 存储态必须基于已认证的用户请求 `UCP_UNAUTHENTICATED_STORED_USER_STATE_LEAK_FORBIDDEN`）及 PR `#900`（`examples.json` 合规语料库导出必须完整并通过扩展组合校验）。

### 59.2 ACP Checkout Request `LineItem` Reference Alignment, Flat `DelegateAuthenticationSessionWithResult` Schema & Inline/Embedded Example Validators (`verify_acp_checkout_line_item_and_delegate_auth_result`, ACP PRs `#270`, `#261`, `#254`, `#252`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#270` (`fix: align checkout request line_items with LineItem`): `CheckoutSessionCreateRequest.line_items` and `CheckoutSessionUpdateRequest.line_items` across `2026-01-30`, `2026-04-17`, and `unreleased` OpenAPI specs must reference `#/components/schemas/LineItem` rather than bare `#/components/schemas/Item`, and every request `line_items[]` entry must carry `id`, nested `item: {id, name, unit_amount}`, `quantity >= 1`, and `totals[]` (`ACP_CHECKOUT_REQUEST_LINE_ITEM_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`). Enforces ACP PR `#261` (flattening `DelegateAuthenticationSessionWithResult` with `additionalProperties: false` so `authentication_result` validates alongside `authentication_session_id` and `status`: `ACP_DELEGATE_AUTH_RESULT_SCHEMA_UNSATISFIABLE_OR_INVALID`) and PRs `#254`/`#252` (validating embedded `$defs`/`components.schemas` examples and OpenAPI `requestBody`/`responses` inline `$ref` examples across all 4 ACP specs).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#270`（对齐结账请求 `line_items` 为 `LineItem`）：`2026-01-30`、`2026-04-17` 与 `unreleased` OpenAPI 中的 `CheckoutSessionCreateRequest.line_items` 和 `CheckoutSessionUpdateRequest.line_items` 必须引用 `#/components/schemas/LineItem` 而非裸 `Item`，且每个条目必须携带 `id`、嵌套 `item`、`quantity >= 1` 与 `totals[]`（`ACP_CHECKOUT_REQUEST_LINE_ITEM_SCHEMA_MISMATCH`）。同时强制执行 PR `#261`（将 `DelegateAuthenticationSessionWithResult` 改为显式包含 `authentication_result` 的扁平 `additionalProperties: false` 对象）与 PR `#254`/`#252`（覆盖 4 大规范的内嵌 `$defs`/`components.schemas` 示例及 OpenAPI `requestBody`/`responses` 内联 `$ref` 示例校验）。

### 59.3 Google AP2 Versioned `.1` VCT Constants, `PaymentInstrument` Extension Preservation, `~~` Multi-Hop dSD-JWT Chain Decoder, EC/JWK Receipt Verification & Redacted Logging (`verify_ap2_vct_instrument_extensions_and_chain_decoder`, AP2 PRs `#349`, `#333`, `#329`, `#351`, `#365`, `#364`)

- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#349` (exact versioned VCT constants `mandate.payment.open.1`, `mandate.checkout.open.1`, `mandate.payment.1`, and `mandate.checkout.1`, rejecting unversioned strings: `AP2_UNVERSIONED_OR_INVALID_MANDATE_VCT`), PRs `#333`/`#329` (`additionalProperties: true` / `ConfigDict(extra='allow')` on `PaymentInstrument` so type-specific extension fields such as x402 `payee_address` and `facilitator` survive `model_dump()` and signing: `AP2_PAYMENT_INSTRUMENT_EXTENSION_FIELDS_DROPPED`), PR `#351` (`sdJwtDecoder.ts` splitting multi-hop dSD-JWT chains on `~~` before single `~` so hop 1 is not misread as hop 0's trailing `kbJwt`: `AP2_DSDJWT_CHAIN_SINGLE_TILDE_SPLIT_CORRUPTION`), PR `#365` (`ReceiptClient.verify_receipt` normalizing `ec.EllipticCurvePublicKey` via `JWK.from_pyca` and verifying signatures before calling `has_reference_in_store_cb`), and PR `#364` (redacting payment tokens, checkout mandates, `risk_data`, and prompts from MCP/A2A logs).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#349`（四大授权模型精确版本化 VCT 常量 `mandate.payment.open.1`、`mandate.checkout.open.1`、`mandate.payment.1`、`mandate.checkout.1`，拦截无版本号旧值 `AP2_UNVERSIONED_OR_INVALID_MANDATE_VCT`）、PR `#333`/`#329`（`PaymentInstrument` 开启 `additionalProperties: true` / `ConfigDict(extra='allow')`，确保 x402 `payee_address` 与 `facilitator` 等类型专属字段在 `model_dump()` 与签名后不丢失 `AP2_PAYMENT_INSTRUMENT_EXTENSION_FIELDS_DROPPED`）、PR `#351`（先按 `~~` 切分多跳委托链再逐跳按 `~` 解码，防止第 2 跳披露项被吞噬 `AP2_DSDJWT_CHAIN_SINGLE_TILDE_SPLIT_CORRUPTION`）、PR `#365`（`ReceiptClient.verify_receipt` 兼容原生 EC 公钥与 JWK 且在回调前完成验签）与 PR `#364`（严禁 MCP/A2A 记录敏感支付载荷日志）。

### 59.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Voice Design API Units (`guidance_scale=5`), Unpinned `voice_design/<lang>.yaml`, Inline Text-Matrix `results.json` Trial Counting & Telecom `ACTION` Reward Basis (`verify_tau2_voice_design_scale_and_text_matrix_inline`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#636`, `#619`, `#616`, `#645`, `#632`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#636` (`setup_voices` must send ElevenLabs API-unit `guidance_scale=5` [valid range `1..20`] rather than passing the UI slider percentage `38`, which produces slow pause-heavy voices that shift benchmark performance by up to 10%: `TAU2_VOICE_DESIGN_UI_PERCENTAGE_SENT_AS_API_SCALE`), PR `#619` (`data/tau2/voice_design/<lang>.yaml` for all 6 `voice-v2` languages `en/es/pt/hi/zh/ko` with `eleven_ttv_v3` personas stored outside `data/tau2/multilingual/` so `pack_sha256` pins remain intact: `TAU2_VOICE_DESIGN_YAML_MISSING_OR_POLLUTES_PACK_PIN`), PR `#616` (`export_submission` passing `cell/results.json` file path rather than cell directory so inline `text-v1` simulations are counted instead of returning `0`: `TAU2_TEXT_MATRIX_DIRECTORY_PATH_DROPS_INLINE_TRIALS`), and PRs `#645`/`#632` (`32` telecom tasks and `9` `banking_knowledge` tasks with `RewardType.ACTION`, plus bounded `<= 1` `factory_draft_repair` call).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#636`（`setup_voices` 必须发送 ElevenLabs API 单位 `guidance_scale=5`［合理区间 `1..20`］，严禁将 UI 滑块百分比 `38` 直接作为 API 参数发送以免合成语音迟缓停顿造成高达 `10%` 评测偏差：`TAU2_VOICE_DESIGN_UI_PERCENTAGE_SENT_AS_API_SCALE`）、PR `#619`（为 `en/es/pt/hi/zh/ko` 六语种在 `data/tau2/multilingual/` 之外提供不污染 `pack_sha256` 锁定的 `voice_design/<lang>.yaml` `eleven_ttv_v3` 配置）、PR `#616`（`export_submission` 必须传入 `cell/results.json` 文件路径而非目录，防止触发 `voice` 目录布局检测而丢失全部内联 `text-v1` 仿真结果：`TAU2_TEXT_MATRIX_DIRECTORY_PATH_DROPS_INLINE_TRIALS`）及 PR `#645`/`#632`（核验 `telecom` 32 个与 `banking_knowledge` 9 个 `RewardType.ACTION` 任务分布及 `tau2 factory` 最多 1 次有界修复）。

## 60. Stage 61 16-Scenario Bilingual (CN/EN) 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`RA-01..RA-16`) (Stage 61 / 中英双语)

- **English**: Evaluates 16 end-to-end scenarios (`RA-01` through `RA-16`) in [`lab/ucp_root_array_order_acp_lineitem_ap2_vct_tau2_voice_design_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_root_array_order_acp_lineitem_ap2_vct_tau2_voice_design_bench.py) across all four Stage 60 guard domains, generating both Chinese ([`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/index.html)) and English ([`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/index.en.html)) 7-Card 4:5 visual decks backed by [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/report.json). The AI-Native 6-Plane pipeline achieves **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** perturbation stability at **0.35 ms** median latency (**13.1x** faster than the **4.595 ms** Classic 3-Tier baseline at **1/16, 6.25%**).
- **中文**: 在 [`lab/ucp_root_array_order_acp_lineitem_ap2_vct_tau2_voice_design_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_root_array_order_acp_lineitem_ap2_vct_tau2_voice_design_bench.py) 中构建覆盖 Stage 60 四大协议域的 16 场景（`RA-01..RA-16`）基准评测，同步生成中文版（[`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/index.html)）与英文版（[`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/index.en.html)）7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告及密封数据 [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-root-array-order-acp-lineitem-ap2-vct-tau2-voice-design-bench/report.json)。AI-Native 6-Plane 流水线实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 顺序反转稳定性，中位内联延迟 **0.35 ms**（较经典三层基线 **4.595 ms / 1/16 6.25%** 提速 **13.1x**）。

## 61. UCP Cart Completion `cart_id` Omission (`#911`/`#910`) & `UCP-Agent` Signature Coverage (`#907`/`#906`/`#883`/`#853`), ACP Store BOPIS Extension (`#171`) & Per-Line Offer Expiry (`#165`/`#162`/`#230`/`#161`), Google AP2 Closed Checkout Binding (`#330`) & x402 Fail-Closed Amount/Key/TrustBoost PII (`#310`/`#300`/`#267`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Active Telecom Refuel (`#611`) & Live-DB Grading / Tool Faults / GPT-5 `tool_choice` (`#609`/`#610`/`#615`/`#621`) (Stage 62 / 中英双语)

### 61.1 UCP Cart-to-Checkout Conversion & Completion Hygiene, `UCP-Agent` Signature Coverage, OpenRPC Auth `type` & Normative Requirements Extractor (`validate_ucp_cart_complete_auth_requirements_guard`, UCP PRs `#911`, `#910`, `#907`, `#906`, `#883`, `#853`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#911` / `#871` (`fix(cart): omit cart_id on complete_checkout requests`) and PR `#910` (`docs(cart): clarify line_items requirement in cart-to-checkout conversion`): `cart_id.ucp_request` must explicitly declare `"complete": "omit"` (`ucp_cart_id_schema_missing_complete_omit`), `complete_checkout` requests carrying `cart_id` are rejected (`ucp_cart_id_forbidden_on_complete_checkout`), and `create_checkout` requests converting a cart via `cart_id` must still pass `line_items` (canonically `"line_items": []`) due to monotonic `allOf` inheritance (`ucp_line_items_required_on_cart_conversion`). Enforces PR `#907` (requiring `"ucp-agent"` in `Signature-Input` covered components whenever a signed request carries `UCP-Agent`: `ucp_signature_coverage_insufficient_ucp_agent`), PR `#906` (`"required": true` on `type` in `ec.auth` and `ep.cart.auth` OpenRPC descriptors), PR `#883` (`dev.ucp.shopping.buyer_consent` and `dev.ucp.common.payment.ap2_mandate` exposed on MCP `complete_checkout`), and PR `#853` (`^REQ-[A-Z0-9-]+-[0-9]{2,}$` <= 42 chars, 10-hex SHA-256 `content_digest`, and `207 + 29 + 23 = 259` clause reconciliation).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#911`/`#871` 与 PR `#910`（购物车转结账与完成阶段边界）：在 `shopping/cart.json#/$defs/checkout` 中为 `cart_id.ucp_request` 显式声明 `"complete": "omit"`，严禁在 `complete_checkout` 请求中携带 `cart_id`（`ucp_cart_id_forbidden_on_complete_checkout`），且在通过 `cart_id` 创建结账时因 `allOf` 单调继承必须保留 `"line_items": []`（`ucp_line_items_required_on_cart_conversion`）。同时强制执行 PR `#907`（当请求包含 `UCP-Agent` 头时，`Signature-Input` 覆盖集必须包含 `"ucp-agent"`）、PR `#906`（OpenRPC `ec.auth` 与 `ep.cart.auth` 的 `type` 参数标记为 `"required": true`）、PR `#883`（MCP `complete_checkout` 声明 `buyer_consent` 与 `ap2_mandate` 扩展）及 PR `#853`（规范条款提取器 `REQ-*` ID、10 位 SHA-256 `content_digest` 与 `207 + 29 + 23 = 259` 守恒对账）。

### 61.2 ACP Delegate Payment Optional `checkout_session_id` & Forward-Compatible Errors, Store BOPIS Extension, Time-Bound Line-Item Offer Expiry & Order Status Semantics (`validate_acp_store_offer_order_delegate_guard`, ACP PRs `#230`, `#171`, `#165`, `#162`, `#161`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#230` (`allowance.checkout_session_id` is optional on `delegate_payment`, while `reason`, `max_amount`, `currency`, `merchant_id`, and `expires_at` remain required) and PR `#161` (`Error.type` / `Error.code` add `unauthorized` [401], `internal_server_error` [500], and `service_unavailable` [503], with RFC §4.2 forward-compatible fallback forbidding strict enum rejection of future codes: `acp_delegate_error_strict_enum_rejection_forbidden`). Enforces PR `#171` (Store Extension for BOPIS: `store_id` allowed on `create`/`update` checkout requests, required `id`+`name` on `CheckoutSession.store`, `1 <= limit <= 100` & `radius > 0` on `GET /stores`, and forbidding `store_id` on `CheckoutSessionCompleteRequest`: `acp_store_id_forbidden_on_checkout_complete`), PR `#165` (per-line `valid_until` vs. session `quote_expires_at`, `offer_valid`/`offer_expired` availability statuses, and `offer_expired` vs. `quote_expired` message codes), and PR `#162` (forbidding `shipped` or physical `delivered` on digital-only orders: `acp_digital_order_forbidden_shipped_status`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#230`（`delegate_payment` 中 `allowance.checkout_session_id` 设为可选，保留其余 5 项必填）与 PR `#161`（扩展 `unauthorized` [401]、`internal_server_error` [500] 与 `service_unavailable` [503] 错误枚举，并遵循 RFC §4.2 前向兼容原则禁止严格枚举拒绝未知错误码）。同时强制执行 PR `#171`（BOPIS 门店扩展：允许在创建/更新结账时传入 `store_id`，但严禁在 `CheckoutSessionCompleteRequest` 传入 `store_id` 以防支付中途重算税费与库存竞态 `acp_store_id_forbidden_on_checkout_complete`）、PR `#165`（区分行级 `LineItem.valid_until` 与会话级 `quote_expires_at`，实施 `offer_valid`/`offer_expired` 状态及 `offer_expired`/`quote_expired` 错误码分流）与 PR `#162`（纯数字商品订单严禁使用物理物流专属的 `shipped` 状态 `acp_digital_order_forbidden_shipped_status`）。

### 61.3 Google AP2 Closed Checkout Binding, x402 PSP Key Fail-Closed, Verified Mandate Amount Authorization & TrustBoost PII Sanitization (`validate_ap2_closed_binding_x402_trustboost_guard`, AP2 PRs `#330`, `#310`, `#300`, `#267`)

- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#330` (`PaymentMandateChain.verify()` must fail closed unless the closed Payment Mandate's `transaction_id` matches the expected Checkout JWT binding, even when `expected_open_checkout_hash` matches: `ap2_closed_mandate_checkout_binding_unverified`), PR `#310` (`x402_psp_mcp.settle_payment` immediately returning `agent_provider_key_missing` when `agent_provider_pub` cannot be loaded instead of skipping SD-JWT verification), PR `#300` (`x402_credentials_provider_mcp` reading `amount_cents` strictly from the verified `closed_mandate.payment_amount.amount` and failing closed if missing, never falling back in `except AttributeError` to a hardcoded `1250` cents: `ap2_x402_hardcoded_1250_amount_fallback_detected`), and PR `#267` (TrustBoost pre-LLM PII sanitization across LATAM `CPF`/`CUIT`/`RFC` + HTTP 402 x402 USDC settlement and Solana `anchor_tx` verification).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#330`（`PaymentMandateChain.verify()` 默认强制校验闭式支付授权 `transaction_id` 与目标 Checkout JWT 的绑定关系，防止仅传 `expected_open_checkout_hash` 时放行绑定到其他结账单的闭式授权 `ap2_closed_mandate_checkout_binding_unverified`）、PR `#310`（x402 PSP `settle_payment` 在缺失 `agent_provider_pub` 公钥时立即以 `agent_provider_key_missing` 故障闭合，阻断跳过验签的静默结算）、PR `#300`（x402 凭证提供方严格按已验签 `closed_mandate.payment_amount.amount` 签发 EIP-3009 授权，严禁在捕获 `AttributeError` 时回退至硬编码 `1250` 美分 `ap2_x402_hardcoded_1250_amount_fallback_detected`）及 PR `#267`（TrustBoost 多语种/拉美 `CPF`/`CUIT`/`RFC` 隐私脱敏、HTTP 402 x402 自动支付与 Solana 主网 `anchor_tx` 存证核验）。

### 61.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Active Telecom Line `refuel_data` Guard, Live-DB Reward Grading, Deterministic `ToolFault` Injection & Bridged GPT-5 `tool_choice` Preservation (`validate_tau2_telecom_livedb_fault_cli_guard`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#611`, `#610`, `#609`, `#615`, `#621`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#611` (`refuel_data` must verify the telecom line is `"active"` before mutating data allowance or billing, rejecting `"suspended"`, `"pending_activation"`, and `"closed"` lines with zero database side effects: `tau2_telecom_inactive_line_refuel_mutation`), PRs `#609`/`#610` (grading environment DB checks against `live_db_hash` / `live_user_db_hash` captured at the end of the run rather than action replay, surfacing `replay_divergence` in `RewardInfo.info`, and enforcing `timeout_before_commit` vs. `timeout_after_commit` `ToolFault` semantics), PR `#615` (`check_tasks` verifying trajectories against the recorded language pack's reviewed task replacements rather than 1.x base registry tasks), and PR `#621` (passing `allowed_openai_params=["tool_choice"]` on bridged `openai/responses/<model>` GPT-5 calls so `tool_choice="required"` is never silently dropped to `None`: `tau2_gpt5_bridged_call_dropped_tool_choice`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#611`（`refuel_data` 必须在修改流量额度与账单前校验电信线路状态为 `"active"`，对 `"suspended"`、`"pending_activation"` 或 `"closed"` 线路零副作用拒止 `tau2_telecom_inactive_line_refuel_mutation`）、PR `#609`/`#610`（以运行结束时的实时环境哈希 `live_db_hash` / `live_user_db_hash` 决定奖励评分，将重放差异记录为 `RewardInfo.info.replay_divergence`，并精确区分 `timeout_before_commit`［跳过工具］与 `timeout_after_commit`［先落库后超时］故障注入语义）、PR `#615`（`check_tasks` 按记录的语言绑定校验 50 个同名替换任务而非 1.x 注册表基线）及 PR `#621`（在桥接 `openai/responses/<model>` 的 GPT-5 调用中显式传入 `allowed_openai_params=["tool_choice"]`，防止 `drop_params=True` 将 `tool_choice="required"` 静默丢弃为 `None`）。

- **English**: Evaluates 16 end-to-end scenarios (`CC-01` through `CC-16`) in [`lab/ucp_cart_complete_acp_store_offer_ap2_x402_tau2_telecom_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_cart_complete_acp_store_offer_ap2_x402_tau2_telecom_bench.py) across all four Stage 62 guard domains, generating both Chinese ([`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/index.html)) and English ([`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/index.en.html)) 7-Card 4:5 visual decks backed by [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/report.json). The AI-Native 6-Plane pipeline achieves **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** perturbation stability at **0.35 ms** median latency (**13.2x** faster than the **4.605 ms** Classic 3-Tier baseline at **1/16, 6.25%**).
- **中文**: 在 [`lab/ucp_cart_complete_acp_store_offer_ap2_x402_tau2_telecom_bench.py`](../../lab/ucp_cart_complete_acp_store_offer_ap2_x402_tau2_telecom_bench.py) 中构建覆盖 Stage 62 四大协议域的 16 场景（`CC-01..CC-16`）基准评测，同步生成中文版（[`index.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/index.html)）与英文版（[`index.en.html`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/index.en.html)）7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告及密封数据 [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-cart-complete-acp-store-offer-ap2-x402-tau2-telecom-bench/report.json)。AI-Native 6-Plane 流水线实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 顺序反转稳定性，中位内联延迟 **0.35 ms**（较经典三层基线 **4.605 ms / 1/16 6.25%** 提速 **13.2x**）。

## 63. UCP Service `schema` Transport Polymorphism (`#833`) & Permalink CSRF / WBA Signing (`#831`/`#791`/`#789`/`#785`), ACP Stripe Link Wallet Handler (`com.stripe.link`, `#156`) & Wallet Intervention Flows (`#150`/`#144`/`#166`/`#151`), Google AP2 Cross-Merchant Atomic Budget Authority (`#252`) & Solana Pay Reference Binding / AP2-Haggle (`#228`/`#229`), and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Incomplete Gold DB State Withdrawal (`#580`) & NL-Judge Verdict / Cost Accounting (`#581`/`#584`/`#593`/`#600`/`#588`) (Stage 64 / 中英双语)

### 63.1 UCP Service `schema` Transport Definition Polymorphism, Reserved `ucp_` Prefix `W007`, Participant Identifier Threat Model, Permalink CSRF Cart Merge Guard & WBA `signature-agent` Coverage (`verify_ucp_service_schema_w007_permalink_and_wba_signing`, UCP PRs `#833`, `#831`, `#791`, `#789`, `#785`)

- **English**: Enforces UCP PR `#833` (`docs(schema): clarify that service schema references a transport definition, not a JSON Schema`): `services[].schema` must reference an OpenAPI 3.1 document (`*.openapi.json`) for `transport="rest"`, an OpenRPC 1.3.2 document (`*.openrpc.json`) for `transport="mcp"` or `"embedded"`, and must be absent for `transport="a2a"` (resolved via the `endpoint` Agent Card), whereas `capabilities[].schema` and `payment_handlers[].schema` reference JSON Schema 2020-12 (`ucp_service_schema_transport_format_mismatch`). Enforces PR `#831` (forbidding non-annotation `ucp_`-prefixed keys such as `$defs/ucp_agent` under `ucp-schema` linter `W007`: `ucp_reserved_prefix_w007_violation`), PR `#791` (rejecting `identity.access_token` as caller authentication per Binding rule 4, requiring `config.business_id`, and forbidding `/tokenize` or `/detokenize` on `encrypted-credential` handlers: `ucp_tokenizer_participant_id_used_as_authentication`), PR `#789` (blocking automatic permalink-initiated cart merge on page load, redirect, or pre-filled submit without explicit buyer action on rendered state: `ucp_permalink_auto_cart_merge_without_buyer_action`), and PR `#785` (requiring both `"ucp-agent"` and `"signature-agent";key="<label>"` in `Signature-Input` when `Signature-Agent` is present, plus `"content-digest"`/`"content-type"` when `body_bytes` is non-empty: `ucp_wba_signature_agent_or_ucp_agent_coverage_missing`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#833`（区分 `service.schema` 传输定义与 JSON Schema）：`services[].schema` 在 `rest` 下必须指向 OpenAPI 3.1（`*.openapi.json`）、在 `mcp`/`embedded` 下指向 OpenRPC 1.3.2（`*.openrpc.json`）、在 `a2a` 下必须缺省（由 `endpoint` Agent Card 解析），而 `capabilities` 与 `payment_handlers` 的 `schema` 则指向 JSON Schema 2020-12（`ucp_service_schema_transport_format_mismatch`）。同时强制执行 PR `#831`（`ucp-schema` `W007` 规则：除 `ucp_request`/`ucp_response` 外严禁使用 `$defs/ucp_agent` 等 `ucp_` 保留前缀）、PR `#791`（`identity.access_token` 仅为参与者标识符而非密钥，严禁 Tokenizer 将其作为调用方认证凭据 `ucp_tokenizer_participant_id_used_as_authentication`）、PR `#789`（阻断在页面加载、重定向或预填自动提交时静默合并购物永久链接至已登录买家购物车的 CSRF 漏洞，必须取得买家对渲染状态的显式操作 `ucp_permalink_auto_cart_merge_without_buyer_action`）与 PR `#785`（WBA 签名请求必须同时覆盖 `"signature-agent";key="<label>"`、`"ucp-agent"` 及非空 `body_bytes` 的摘要头）。

### 63.2 ACP Stripe Link Accelerated Wallet Handler (`com.stripe.link`), Wallet Intervention Flows (`redirect`/`webview`/`otp`), All 11 Session States, On-Chain Discount & Intent Traces (`verify_acp_stripe_link_intervention_session_states_and_discounts`, ACP PRs `#156`, `#150`, `#144`, `#166`, `#151`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#156` (`SEP: Payment handler for Stripe link` — `com.stripe.link`): the buyer authenticates directly with Link (email + OTP), Link returns a scoped wallet token, and the agent delegates it via `delegate_payment` to obtain a `vt_...`/`spt_...` token without exposing raw PAN/CVC to the agent or seller (`acp_link_handler_raw_pan_exposure_forbidden` / `acp_link_handler_unauthenticated_or_undelegated_token`). Enforces PR `#150` (adding `redirect`, `webview`, and `otp` to `InterventionCapabilities`, requiring unexpired `InterventionContext` on `authentication_required`/`intervention_required`, and matching `InterventionResponse.type` in `{"otp", "redirect_complete", "webview_complete"}`), PR `#144` (all 11 `CheckoutSessionBase.status` values, blocking duplicate `complete` calls while `complete_in_progress`: `acp_duplicate_complete_while_complete_in_progress`, and enforcing terminal state immutability on `completed`/`canceled`/`expired`), PR `#166` (on-chain token-eligibility single-use discount `duration="once"`, <= 30-min TTL, and per-item `allocations[]` sum invariant), and PR `#151` (10 canonical `intent_trace.reason_code` values on cancel).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#156`（Stripe Link 加速结账钱包处理器 `com.stripe.link`：买家直接通过邮箱 + OTP 向 Link 认证并生成钱包令牌，Agent 经 `delegate_payment` 换取 `vt_...`/`spt_...` 令牌，严禁向 Agent 或卖家暴露原始卡号/CVC `acp_link_handler_raw_pan_exposure_forbidden`）。同时强制执行 PR `#150`（扩展钱包认证干预类型 `redirect`、`webview` 与 `otp`，校验未过期的 `InterventionContext` 及匹配的 `InterventionResponse`）、PR `#144`（完整 11 种会话状态语义，拦截 `complete_in_progress` 期间的重复 `complete` 请求 `acp_duplicate_complete_while_complete_in_progress`，并锁定 `completed`/`canceled`/`expired` 终态不可变）、PR `#166`（链上持仓资格单次折扣 `duration="once"`、30 分钟有效期及行级 `allocations[]` 分摊守恒）与 PR `#151`（取消结账 `intent_trace` 的 10 种标准原因码）。

### 63.3 Google AP2 Cross-Merchant Atomic Budget Authority, Solana Pay Single-Use Reference Pubkey Binding & AP2-Haggle Multi-Round Negotiation (`verify_ap2_cross_merchant_budget_solana_pay_and_haggle`, AP2 PRs `#252`, `#228`, `#229`)

- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#252` (solving `#207` where independent per-merchant `BudgetEvaluator` instances allow `$120` spending on a `$100` budget: requires an external atomic Budget Authority with `authorize`, `commit`, `refund`, and `query` verbs that atomically checks and holds budget before `PaymentMandate` execution: `ap2_cross_merchant_independent_evaluator_overspend` / `ap2_budget_authority_hold_exceeded_or_unauthorized`), PR `#228` (binding human-present Solana USDC transfers deterministically to a signed `PaymentMandate` via a fresh single-use read-only `reference` pubkey in the Solana Pay URL and verifying on-chain `recipient`, `amount`, and `spl_mint`: `ap2_solana_pay_reference_pubkey_reused_or_mismatched`), and PR `#229` (`ap2haggle/ap2/extensions/haggle/v0.1`: forbidding unsigned intermediate `Offer` payloads from authorizing payment, enforcing `round_number <= max_rounds` and buyer `walk_away` / seller `cost_floor` bounds, and requiring a merchant-signed `CartMandate` on accepted `NegotiationOutcome`: `ap2_haggle_unsigned_offer_or_constraint_violation`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#252`（解决 `#207` 多商户独立 `BudgetEvaluator` 并发超支 `$120 > $100` 漏洞：引入具备 `authorize`、`commit`、`refund`、`query` 四大原子原语的外部预算权威，在支付执行前原子校验并冻结预算额度 `ap2_budget_authority_hold_exceeded_or_unauthorized`）、PR `#228`（Solana Pay 在场 USDC 结算绑定：每次结账生成单次只读 `reference` 公钥嵌入 Solana Pay URL，并在链上核验 `recipient`、`amount`、`spl_mint` 与 `PaymentMandate` 完全一致 `ap2_solana_pay_reference_pubkey_reused_or_mismatched`）及 PR `#229`（`AP2-Haggle` 多轮议价扩展：严禁将未签名的中间 `Offer` 用于支付授权，校验轮次上限与买方底线/卖方成本底线，且仅在 `NegotiationOutcome` 产出商户签名 `CartMandate` 后方可进入标准 AP2 支付路径）。

### 63.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Incomplete Gold DB State Withdrawal, NL-Judge Verdict Count & Contentless Turn Filtering, Separate `evaluator_cost` Accounting & Telecom/Sandbox Guards (`verify_tau2_gold_replay_nl_judge_cost_and_sandbox`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#580`, `#581`, `#584`, `#593`, `#600`, `#588`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#580` (`Closes #499`: when a state-mutating golden action raises during reference replay — such as retail tasks `64` and `105` failing on `exchange_delivered_order_items` due to `Insufficient gift card balance` `$21.10 > $17.00` — the evaluator must withdraw DB certification by setting `db_check=None` and omitting `RewardType.DB` from `reward_breakdown` rather than awarding `db_match=True` to no-op trajectories, while keeping write mismatches at `db_reward=0.0` and leaving read-only reference failures intact: `tau2_incomplete_gold_db_state_false_certification`), PR `#581` (`Fixes #554`: rejecting NL-assertion judge replies where `len(verdicts) != len(nl_assertions)` so `all([]) == True` never awards full credit on empty `{"results": []}`: `tau2_nl_judge_verdict_count_mismatch`), PR `#584` (`Fixes #553`: omitting contentless tool-calling assistant turns so literal `"assistant: None"` lines never pollute the NL judge prompt), PR `#593` (`Fixes #556`: persisting `evaluator_cost` and `evaluator_usage` separately from `agent_cost` and `user_cost`), PR `#600` (`Fixes #576`: telecom `TOOL_CALL_GROUNDING` referencing exposed user tool `check_status_bar` instead of `get_status_bar`), and PR `#588` (`Fixes #586`: stopping timed-out `srt` commands with `SIGTERM` before `SIGKILL` so `socat` proxy processes do not leak).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#580`（修复 `#499`：当参考轨迹中的状态变更工具［如 retail 任务 `64` 与 `105` 的 `exchange_delivered_order_items` 因礼品卡余额不足 `$21.10 > $17.00` 抛错］重放失败导致黄金数据库状态未建成时，撤回虚假的 `db_match=True` 认证并设为 `db_check=None`，防止未修改数据库的空操作轨迹白拿满分，同时保留只读黄金动作失败的警告与真实写偏离的 `0.0` 判罚 `tau2_incomplete_gold_db_state_false_certification`）、PR `#581`（修复 `#554`：当 NL 断言裁判返回的裁决数与断言数不一致时抛错重试，阻断 Python `all([]) == True` 对空列表 `{"results": []}` 误判满分 `tau2_nl_judge_verdict_count_mismatch`）、PR `#584`（修复 `#553`：在构建 NL 裁判提示词时过滤 `content=None` 的纯工具调用轮次，消除占零售轨迹 `19.9%` 的 `"assistant: None"` 噪音）、PR `#593`（修复 `#556`：在 `RewardInfo` 与 `SimulationRun` 中独立持久化占真实总成本 `32.8%` 的 `evaluator_cost` 与 `evaluator_usage`）、PR `#600`（修正 2,285 个电信任务的用户模拟器工具锚定为 `check_status_bar`）与 PR `#588`（沙箱命令超时先发 `SIGTERM` 优雅清理两个 `socat` 代理进程，5 秒后再发 `SIGKILL`）。

## 64. Stage 65 16-Scenario Bilingual 7-Card 4:5 Benchmark Suite (`SL-01..SL-16` / Stage 65 16 场景中英双语 7-Card 4:5 基准评测)

- **English**: `lab/ucp_service_schema_acp_link_intervention_ap2_budget_tau2_nl_bench.py` evaluates 16 deterministic scenarios (`SL-01..SL-16`) across Stage 64 protocol guards:
  - `SL-01..SL-04`: UCP `#833` `service.schema` transport definition polymorphism (`rest` OpenAPI 3.1 vs. `mcp`/`embedded` OpenRPC 1.3.2 vs. `a2a` Agent Card), `#831` `W007` `$defs/agent` reserved prefix, `#791` `identity.access_token` non-authentication binding rule, `#789` permalink CSRF cart-merge confirmation, and `#785` RFC 9421 WBA `"signature-agent"` + `"ucp-agent"` signature coverage.
  - `SL-05..SL-08`: ACP `#156` Stripe Link (`com.stripe.link`) email+OTP wallet delegation without raw PAN/CVC exposure, `#150` `redirect`/`webview`/`otp` intervention context, `#144` 11-state checkout lifecycle & `complete_in_progress` duplicate `complete` blocking, `#166` `duration="once"` on-chain token discount allocation conservation, and `#151` `intent_trace.reason_code`.
  - `SL-09..SL-12`: Google AP2 `#252` cross-merchant atomic Budget Authority (`authorize`/`commit`/`refund`/`query`), `#228` Solana Pay single-use read-only `reference` pubkey binding, and `#229` `AP2-Haggle` (`ap2haggle/ap2/extensions/haggle/v0.1`) non-binding intermediate `Offer` & merchant-signed `CartMandate`.
  - `SL-13..SL-16`: Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `#580` incomplete gold DB state withdrawal (`db_check=None` on gift-card replay failure), `#581` NL-judge verdict count verification (`len(verdicts) == len(nl_assertions)`), `#584` omitting `"assistant: None"` turns, `#593` separate `evaluator_cost` persistence, `#600` `check_status_bar` grounding, and `#588` `SIGTERM` cleanup before `SIGKILL`.
  - **Headline Finding**: AI-Native deterministic guards achieve **16/16 (100.0%)** accuracy and **100.0%** order-reversal stability vs. **4/16 (25.0%)** for the Classic 3-Tier baseline, with median latency **0.34 ms** vs. **4.63 ms** (**13.6x** speedup), exported to `reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-service-schema-acp-link-intervention-ap2-budget-tau2-nl-bench/{report.json,index.html,index.en.html}`.
- **中文**: `lab/ucp_service_schema_acp_link_intervention_ap2_budget_tau2_nl_bench.py` 构建覆盖 Stage 64 四大护栏域的 16 个确定性评测场景（`SL-01..SL-16`）：AI-Native 护栏实现 **16/16 (100.0%)** 准确率与 **100.0%** 逆序扰动稳定性（对比经典三层架构基线 **4/16, 25.0%**），中位延迟 **0.34 ms vs. 4.63 ms（13.6x 加速）**，并生成中英双语自包含 7-Card 4:5 视觉评测报告 [`index.html`（中文）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-service-schema-acp-link-intervention-ap2-budget-tau2-nl-bench/index.html) 与 [`index.en.html`（English）](../../reports/agentic-commerce-ucp-service-schema-acp-link-intervention-ap2-budget-tau2-nl-bench/index.en.html)。

---

## 65. UCP Permalink `ucp/version` & Token Context / State-Bound Cart Offer / OpenAPI `securitySchemes`, ACP `session_context` Telemetry & Embedded `app_links` / Post-Checkout Order Alignment, Google AP2 `(type, id)` Instrument Matching & Golden Vectors, and Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` Abort & Fenced NL JSON Guards (Stage 66 / 中英双语架构规范)

### 65.1 UCP Permalink `ucp/version` Control Parameter, `TokenCredential` `binding`/`identity` Context, State-Bound Cart `Offer` in `actions`, Terminal Cart `status`, `action.ready` `maxProperties: 0`, `digital_delivery` Order Pointer & OpenAPI 3.1 `securitySchemes` (`verify_ucp_permalink_version_token_context_cart_offer_and_security_schemes`, UCP PRs `#793`, `#792`, `#773`, `#740`, `#730`, `#719`, `#716`)

- **English**: Enforces Universal Commerce Protocol PR `#793` (requiring exactly one `ucp/version=YYYY-MM-DD` control parameter on every generated permalink carrying UCP field-path query parameters, forbidding pre-release identifiers, consuming `ucp/version` and `continue_to` on resolution so they never leak onto the redirect URL, and never returning a JSON `version_unsupported` error to a browser navigation: `ucp_permalink_version_missing_or_leaked_on_redirect`), PR `#792` (preserving optional `binding` and `identity` on `TokenCredential` unchanged through checkout, comparing presented `binding` at the processor against issuance records, and never accepting `identity` as caller authentication: `ucp_token_credential_binding_mutated_or_mismatch`), PR `#773` (representing available Cart Offers in the Cart `actions` map rather than a parallel `offers.available[]` array, binding each Offer to `applies_to.cart_id` + `applies_to.state_ref`, and keeping `id` + `revision` immutable: `ucp_cart_offer_state_ref_mismatch_or_parallel_array`), PR `#740` (adding immutable terminal cart `status` in `{"ordered", "canceled", "expired"}` alongside `"active"`: `ucp_cart_terminal_status_invalid_or_mutated`), PR `#730` (constraining `action.ready` success branch with `maxProperties: 0` so only `{}` matches success: `ucp_payment_action_ready_non_empty_success_object`), PR `#719` (`com.mercadopago.shopping.digital_delivery` Order extension storing only durable `reference` + `lookup` pointer without caching mutable balance or releasing settlement funds on delivery: `ucp_digital_delivery_cached_balance_or_premature_settlement`), and PR `#716` (`Closes #668`: declaring REST authentication via `components.securitySchemes` instead of OpenAPI 3.1 ignored header parameters `Authorization`, `Accept`, `Content-Type`, and `X-API-Key`: `ucp_openapi_ignored_auth_header_parameter_declared`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 UCP PR `#793`（携带 UCP 字段路径参数的购物永久链接必须包含唯一 `ucp/version=YYYY-MM-DD` 控制参数、严禁预发布版本号、解析后消费控制参数禁泄漏至重定向 URL，且浏览器导航严禁返回 JSON `version_unsupported` 错误 `ucp_permalink_version_missing_or_leaked_on_redirect`）、PR `#792`（在 `TokenCredential` 中透传 `binding` 与 `identity` 上下文，处理器比对签发记录且严禁将 `identity` 当作调用方认证 `ucp_token_credential_binding_mutated_or_mismatch`）、PR `#773`（在 Cart `actions` 映射中表达状态绑定 `Offer`、禁平行 `offers.available[]` 数组，并绑定 `applies_to.cart_id` + `applies_to.state_ref` 与不可变 `revision`）、PR `#740`（Cart 终态 `ordered`/`canceled`/`expired` 不可变）、PR `#730`（`action.ready` 成功分支 `maxProperties: 0` 仅接受 `{}`）、PR `#719`（`com.mercadopago.shopping.digital_delivery` 挂载于持久化 `Order` 且仅存 `reference` + `lookup` 指针、禁缓存可变余额且交付不等于资金结算）及 PR `#716`（OpenAPI 3.1 通过 `components.securitySchemes` 声明认证并移除被规范忽略的 `Authorization`/`Accept`/`Content-Type`/`X-API-Key` 请求头参数）。

### 65.2 ACP `session_context` Telemetry Unification, Embedded `app_links` Deep Links, Post-Checkout 3-Field Order Alignment & Seller-Backed / `x402` Crypto Handlers (`verify_acp_session_context_app_links_order_alignment_and_seller_backed`, ACP PRs `#238`, `#237`, `#232`, `#149`, `#114`, `#111`)

- **English**: Enforces ACP PR `#238` (renaming `complete_checkout` raw buyer/device telemetry object to `session_context` and adding optional `delegate_payment.session_context` while keeping `delegate_payment.risk_signals` strictly as the array of normalized fraud assessments `type`/`score`/`action`: `acp_session_context_or_risk_signals_schema_collision`), PR `#237` (SEP `#229`: priority-ordered `app_links` on `Product` and `Variant` with deterministic fallback to canonical `url`: `acp_feed_app_links_invalid_or_missing_url_fallback`), PR `#232` (SEP `#233`: 3-field line-item quantity conservation `ordered == fulfilled + canceled` and `returned <= fulfilled`, plus order-level `completed` status instead of overloaded `fulfilled`: `acp_order_post_checkout_quantity_or_status_violation`), and PRs `#149`, `#114`, `#111` (`content_attribution` `https://` cited URLs, `dev.acp.seller_backed.{saved_card,gift_card,points,store_credit}` requiring `requires_delegate_payment=True`, `requires_pci_compliance=False`, zero credential transfer to the agent, `points_balance >= purchase_amount`, and verified `x402` crypto facilitator settlement: `acp_seller_backed_or_x402_crypto_handler_violation`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 ACP PR `#238`（将原始买家/设备遥测对象统一命名为 `session_context` 并消除与 `delegate_payment.risk_signals` 标准化欺诈评估数组的命名冲突 `acp_session_context_or_risk_signals_schema_collision`）、PR `#237`（`Product`/`Variant` 优先级 `app_links` 原生/内嵌应用深链及向规范 `url` 的安全回退）、PR `#232`（结账后订单 3 字段数量守恒 `ordered == fulfilled + canceled`、`returned <= fulfilled` 及订单级 `completed` 状态去重载）以及 PR `#149`/`#114`/`#111`（`content_attribution` 引用 URL 归因、`dev.acp.seller_backed.*` 卖家侧托管支付免原始凭证传输与积分余额校验，以及 `x402` 加密货币 Facilitator 验签结算）。

### 65.3 Google AP2 `(type, id)` Allowed Payment Instrument Matching, Versioned `delegate-SD-JWT` Golden Vectors & TypeScript/Zod Receipt Branch Invariants (`verify_ap2_instrument_type_id_golden_vectors_and_zod_sdk`, AP2 PRs `#301`, `#307`, `#305`, `#216`)

- **English**: Enforces Google AP2 PR `#301` (`Fixes #299` item 2: `AllowedPaymentInstrumentEvaluator.evaluate` must match both `allowed.type == instrument.type and allowed.id == instrument.id` so `{id: "pi-1", type: "bank"}` never satisfies an allowed `{id: "pi-1", type: "card"}` constraint: `ap2_allowed_payment_instrument_type_mismatch`), PR `#307` (`Addresses #303`: verifying compact-serialization wire invariants across the 4 frozen `delegate_sd_jwt_vectors.json` chains — an $N$-segment chain has `N - 1` `~~` joins, ends with `~`, and passes deterministic JWS verification: `ap2_delegate_sd_jwt_golden_vector_wire_violation`), PR `#305` (`Addresses #67`: `@ap2-protocol/sdk` TypeScript/Zod generator preserving branch-specific `required` fields on `PaymentReceipt`/`CheckoutReceipt` root `oneOf` — `psp_confirmation_id` + `network_confirmation_id` on `success` vs. `error` + `error_description` on `error`: `ap2_receipt_oneof_branch_required_fields_missing`), and PR `#216` (`ghost-protocol` Base Mainnet `chain_id=8453` EIP-712 non-custodial `IntentMandate` monthly USDC cap enforcement: `ap2_autonomous_infra_intent_mandate_cap_or_sig_invalid`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Google AP2 PR `#301`（修复 `#299`：`AllowedPaymentInstrumentEvaluator` 必须同时匹配 `(type, id)` 二元组，阻断同 `id="pi-1"` 但不同 `type="bank" != "card"` 的支付工具越权通过 `ap2_allowed_payment_instrument_type_mismatch`）、PR `#307`（固化 4 组 `delegate_sd_jwt_vectors.json` 黄金向量的线格式不变量：$N$ 段委托链必含 $N-1$ 个 `~~` 分隔符、以 `~` 结尾且通过 JWS 验签）、PR `#305`（`@ap2-protocol/sdk` TypeScript/Zod 收据根级 `oneOf` 分支必填项保留：成功分支必填 `psp_confirmation_id` + `network_confirmation_id`，错误分支必填 `error` + `error_description`）与 PR `#216`（`ghost-protocol` Base 主网 `8453` EIP-712 非托管自治基础设施 `IntentMandate` 月度 USDC 限额门禁）。

### 65.4 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` Abort Classification, Fenced Markdown JSON NL-Judge Parsing, Deferred Checkpoint Unlink & Banking/Airline/Campus Invariants (`verify_tau2_out_of_scope_abort_fenced_nl_json_checkpoint_and_campus`, Sierra `tau2-bench` PRs `#548`, `#562`, `#533`, `#606`, `#602`, `#596`)

- **English**: Enforces Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#548` (`Fixes #517`: classifying `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` user-simulator aborts before `###STOP###` as `TerminationReason.OUT_OF_SCOPE` across `initialize()`, `step()`, and `full_duplex_orchestrator._process_participant` so premature-termination Rule 5 assigns `0.0` reward rather than grading an aborted simulation as `USER_STOP`: `tau2_out_of_scope_abort_conflated_with_user_stop`), PR `#562` (`Fixes #555`: extracting fenced Markdown JSON blocks via `extract_json_from_llm_response` in standard and full-duplex NL-assertion evaluators while still raising on malformed JSON: `tau2_nl_judge_fenced_json_or_malformed_swallowed`), PR `#533` (deferring removal of the previous directory-format checkpoint trajectory and index entry until the replacement trajectory write succeeds: `tau2_checkpoint_pre_unlink_data_loss_on_write_failure`), and PRs `#606`, `#602`, `#596` (synchronizing all 97 aggregate `banking_knowledge/tasks.json` definitions including task `102` `NL_ASSERTION`, enforcing lowercase airline `user_id`, and validating the Chinese university `campus` dual-control domain on `v1.0.1` scoring `reward_basis = [DB, COMMUNICATE]` + `env_assertions` without `ACTION`: `tau2_banking_aggregate_or_airline_campus_invariant_violation`).
- **中文**: 强制执行 Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 PR `#548`（修复 `#517`：在 `initialize()`、实时运行路径 `step()` 与全双工编排器 `_process_participant` 中将用户模拟器 `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` 优先于 `###STOP###` 归类为 `TerminationReason.OUT_OF_SCOPE` 并按规则 5 判 `0.0` 分，阻断将中止仿真误作 `USER_STOP` 正常打分 `tau2_out_of_scope_abort_conflated_with_user_stop`）、PR `#562`（修复 `#555`：NL 断言评估器通过 `extract_json_from_llm_response` 解析 Markdown 代码块包裹的 JSON 并对损坏 JSON 保持抛错）、PR `#533`（目录格式检查点替换时延迟至新轨迹写入并 `os.replace` 成功后才删除旧轨迹与索引）及 PR `#606`/`#602`/`#596`（同步 `banking_knowledge/tasks.json` 全部 97 个聚合任务定义并补齐任务 `102` 缺失的 `NL_ASSERTION`、强制航空域 `user_id` 全小写、以及校验中国高校教务双控域 `campus` 的 `v1.0.1` `[DB, COMMUNICATE]` + `env_assertions` 评分基线）。
